## Assessment

Both line-by-line cleanup passes are complete. The main sequence of travel, recruitment, the wyvern encounter, capture and the later negotiation is legible, but uncertain geography, NPC/place identities, exact combat choices, mirror procedure and who knows about the scepter remain material. Preserve the marked wording as uncertainty; do not use it as an unqualified factual or canonical claim. Review is deferred to the consolidated morning inventory at the user's instruction. No human acceptance has been recorded. Unmarked incidental fragments are separately inventoried and do not establish additional actions or identities.
